> * **deep-prop-construction-defect-insp** (HANDOVER\_INSPECTION\_LIST.md): Cẩm nang kiểm tra chất lượng bàn giao căn hộ chung cư và nhà xây sẵn: kiểm tra độ phẳng tường/sàn, độ dốc thoát sàn ban công – nhà tắm, rò thấm cổ ống, áp lực nước, tiếp địa ổ cắm điện và đo diện tích thông thủy thực tế.

## **69\. Nông nghiệp Công nghệ cao, Thú y, Thủy sản & An toàn Thực phẩm (AgTech, Aquaculture & Food Safety)**

> * **deep-agro-soil-npk-fertigation** (SOIL\_NUTRITION\_PLAN.md): Khoa học đất và dinh dưỡng cây trồng: đọc kết quả phân tích đất (*pH*, EC, mùn hữu cơ, dung lượng trao đổi cation CEC), cân đối công thức phân bón đa – trung – vi lượng theo từng giai đoạn sinh trưởng và tưới nhỏ giọt châm phân tự động.<br>
> * **deep-agro-hydroponics-cea-climate** (CEA\_HYDROPONIC\_RECIPE.md): Kỹ thuật thủy canh, khí canh và nhà màng điều khiển khí hậu chính xác: pha dung dịch dinh dưỡng chuẩn phần triệu (ppm) không kết tủa muối, kiểm soát chênh lệch áp suất hơi nước (VPD) và phổ đèn LED quang hợp.<br>
> * **deep-agro-ipm-crop-pathology** (IPM\_DISEASE\_PROTOCOL.md): Quản lý dịch hại tổng hợp (IPM) và bệnh học thực vật: chẩn đoán triệu chứng nấm, vi khuẩn, virus, côn trùng chích hút và kết hợp thiên địch sinh học với luân phiên hoạt chất tránh kháng thuốc, đảm bảo thời gian cách ly.<br>
> * **deep-agro-precision-drone-ndvi** (PRECISION\_AG\_WORKFLOW.md): Nông nghiệp chính xác ứng dụng viễn thám và thiết bị không người lái: phân tích bản đồ chỉ số thực vật (NDVI/NDRE) phát hiện sớm vùng cây suy yếu và lập bản đồ phun/bón biến thiên đúng liều lượng từng ô ruộng.<br>
> * **deep-agro-aquaculture-water-biofloc** (AQUACULTURE\_WATER\_LOG.md): Kỹ thuật nuôi trồng thủy sản thâm canh bền vững (tôm, cá tuần hoàn RAS / Biofloc): kiểm soát chặt oxy hòa tan (DO), độ kiềm, chu trình chuyển hóa khí độc NH3​→NO2−​→NO3−​, hệ số chuyển đổi thức ăn (FCR) và an toàn sinh học ao nuôi.<br>
> * **deep-agro-livestock-vet-husbandry** (LIVESTOCK\_BIOSECURITY.md): Khoa học chăn nuôi và vệ sinh phòng dịch thú y: tối ưu khẩu phần thức ăn theo nhu cầu axit amin – năng lượng trao đổi, thiết kế chuồng trại thông thoáng mát, lịch tiêm phòng vaccine và quy trình cách ly an toàn dịch bệnh.<br>
> * **deep-agro-forestry-agroecology** (AGROFORESTRY\_DESIGN.md): Nông lâm kết hợp (Agroforestry), canh tác tái sinh đất đa tầng tán, tính toán trữ lượng hấp thụ carbon rừng trồng và chọn giống cây bản địa thích ứng hạn mặn.<br>
> * **deep-agro-postharvest-cold-storage** (POSTHARVEST\_HANDLING.md): Công nghệ sau thu hoạch nông sản xuất khẩu: xử lý làm mát sơ bộ nhanh (pre-cooling), điều chỉnh khí quyển bảo quản (CA/MAP), kiểm soát khí Ethylene làm chín và kéo dài tuổi thọ vận chuyển đường biển.<br>
> * **deep-agro-food-haccp-iso22000** (HACCP\_CCP\_PLAN.md): Xây dựng hệ thống quản lý an toàn thực phẩm nhà máy chế biến theo chuẩn HACCP, ISO 22000, BRC và GlobalG.A.P.: phân tích mối nguy sinh – hóa – lý và giám sát điểm kiểm soát tới hạn (CCP).<br>
> * **deep-agro-commodity-traceability** (TRACEABILITY\_LOT\_SCHEMA.json): Hệ thống truy xuất nguồn gốc nông sản – thực phẩm từ mã số vùng trồng, nhật ký canh tác điện tử, nhà máy đóng gói đến mã QR trên kệ siêu thị đáp ứng tiêu chuẩn kiểm dịch quốc tế.

## **70\. Chiến thuật Cờ, Trò chơi Đối kháng, Esports & Cân bằng Game (Chess/Go, GTO, Esports & Game Balance)**

> * **deep-tactics-chess-calculation-end** (CHESS\_ANALYSIS\_TREE.pgn): Huấn luyện Cờ vua chuyên sâu: phương pháp tính biến cưỡng bức (*Checks, Captures, Threats*), đánh giá cấu trúc tốt/ô yếu, xây dựng kho khai cuộc nhất quán và kỹ thuật tàn cuộc chuẩn xác lý thuyết.<br>
> * **deep-tactics-xiangqi-go-positional** (BOARD\_STRATEGY\_NOTES.md): Nghệ thuật Cờ tướng (khai cuộc tiên/hậu, sát pháp trung cuộc, cờ tàn thực chiến) và Cờ vây (cân bằng thế lực – đất thực, sống chết nhóm quân, cảm giác hình cờ và thu quan chuẩn xác từng mục).<br>
> * **deep-tactics-poker-gto-imperfect** (GTO\_RANGE\_EV\_ANALYSIS.md): Lý thuyết trò chơi thông tin không đầy đủ và xác suất kỳ vọng (Expected Value): xây dựng dải bài cân bằng (GTO ranges), tỷ lệ cược pot odds, tần suất phòng thủ tối thiểu (MDF) và điều chỉnh khai thác sai lầm đối thủ.<br>
> * **deep-tactics-esports-vod-macro** (VOD\_MACRO\_REVIEW.md): Phân tích băng ghi hình thi đấu Esports chuyên nghiệp (MOBA, Tactical FPS, RTS): kiểm soát tầm nhìn bản đồ, nhịp độ di chuyển mục tiêu lớn, quản lý kinh tế vòng đấu và giao tiếp thông tin ngắn gọn của đội hình.<br>
> * **deep-tactics-draft-meta-counter** (META\_DRAFT\_MATRIX.md): Phân tích xu hướng chiến thuật bản cập nhật (Meta-game), xây dựng ma trận cấm/chọn đội hình tương khắc (Drafting synergy & counter-picks) và chuẩn bị bài đánh bất ngờ trong loạt trận BO3/BO5.<br>
> * **deep-tactics-game-economy-sinks** (ECONOMY\_FAUCET\_SINK.md): Thiết kế và mô phỏng nền kinh tế trong trò chơi trực tuyến: cân bằng vòi bơm tài nguyên (faucets) và phễu tiêu thụ (sinks) ngăn chặn siêu lạm phát tiền tệ, thiết kế bảng xác suất rơi vật phẩm (drop tables/pity timers).<br>
> * **deep-tactics-combat-frame-balance** (COMBAT\_BALANCE\_SHEET.md): Cân bằng toán học hệ thống chiến đấu trong game: đường cong sát thương/chống chịu theo cấp độ, dữ liệu khung hình đòn đánh (startup, active, recovery frames) và loại bỏ chiến thuật bá đạo phá hỏng tính cạnh tranh.<br>
> * **deep-tactics-matchmaking-elo-glicko** (MATCHMAKING\_RATING\_SPEC.md): Thiết kế thuật toán xếp hạng kỹ năng và ghép trận công bằng (Elo, Glicko-2, TrueSkill): xử lý độ lệch không chắc chắn của người chơi mới, tổ đội chênh bậc và tối ưu thời gian chờ hàng đợi.<br>
> * **deep-tactics-tabletop-board-design** (BOARDGAME\_RULEBOOK\_SPEC.md): Thiết kế trò chơi bàn cờ (Board Games & Card Games): tạo vòng lặp quyết định thú vị tránh người dẫn đầu thắng chắc quá sớm (runaway leader), viết sách luật chặt chẽ không kẽ hở và kiểm thử mù.<br>
> * **deep-tactics-puzzle-level-pacing** (LEVEL\_DESIGN\_FLOW.md): Thiết kế màn chơi và câu đố thông minh: dạy cơ chế mới thông qua trải nghiệm trực quan không cần chữ dài dòng, dẫn hướng ánh nhìn bằng kiến trúc môi trường và kiểm soát nhịp căng – chùng hợp lý.

## **71\. Đời sống Gia đình, Sửa chữa Nhà cửa DIY, Chăm sóc Xe & Kỹ năng Sinh tồn (Family, DIY, Auto & Preparedness)**

> * **deep-life-parenting-child-dev** (FAMILY\_ROUTINES\_GUIDE.md): Đồng hành nuôi dạy con theo từng giai đoạn phát triển tâm sinh lý: giao tiếp kết nối vừa ấm áp vừa giữ kỷ luật nhất quán, khơi gợi tư duy tự lập và thiết lập thói quen đọc sách, sử dụng thiết bị số lành mạnh.<br>
> * **deep-life-eldercare-family-coord** (ELDERCARE\_SAFETY\_PLAN.md): Tổ chức chăm sóc người cao tuổi trong gia đình chu đáo: cải tạo không gian sống chống trượt ngã, lịch nhắc thuốc/khám định kỳ rõ ràng, sổ hồ sơ sức khỏe tập trung và chia sẻ trách nhiệm giữa các thành viên.<br>
> * **deep-life-home-electrical-plumbing** (HOME\_REPAIR\_SAFETY\_STEPS.md): Chẩn đoán và tự sửa chữa điện – nước dân dụng an toàn tại nhà: nguyên tắc ngắt nguồn kiểm tra bút thử điện/đồng hồ vạn năng trước tiên, xử lý nhảy aptomat chống giật (RCBO), thay van vòi rò nước, thông tắc và chống thấm cơ bản.<br>
> * **deep-life-appliance-hvac-trouble** (APPLIANCE\_DIAGNOSTIC\_TREE.md): Bắt bệnh và bảo dưỡng định kỳ thiết bị gia dụng (điều hòa kém lạnh/chảy nước, máy giặt báo lỗi thoát/cấp nước, tủ lạnh đóng tuyết, máy lọc nước RO yếu áp) phân biệt rõ việc tự vệ sinh được với việc cần gọi thợ chuyên dụng.<br>
> * **deep-life-woodworking-maker-diy** (MAKER\_BUILD\_CUTLIST.md): Thiết kế và chế tác đồ gỗ, kệ bàn góc làm việc và dự án DIY tại nhà: lập bản vẽ tối ưu cắt ván ít hao hụt, chọn kết cấu mộng/vít chịu lực không võng, đi dây cáp gọn gàng và an toàn dụng cụ cắt mài.<br>
> * **deep-life-auto-moto-diagnostics** (VEHICLE\_MAINTENANCE\_LOG.md): Bảo dưỡng và đọc bệnh ô tô, xe máy cá nhân: giải mã mã lỗi OBD-II, lịch thay dầu nhớt/nước làm mát/dầu phanh/lốp/ắc quy chuẩn số km, nhận diện tiếng kêu gầm máy bất thường để không bị báo giá thay phụ tùng oan.<br>
> * **deep-life-garment-textile-care** (TEXTILE\_STAIN\_PROTOCOL.md): Khoa học chăm sóc trang phục, giày da và chất liệu vải kỹ thuật: xử lý hóa học từng loại vết bẩn cứng đầu (dầu mỡ, mực, cà phê, máu, ố vàng) theo đúng chất sợi vải mà không làm bạc màu hay co rút.<br>
> * **deep-life-pet-behavior-care** (PET\_CARE\_PROTOCOL.md): Chăm sóc và huấn luyện thú cưng (chó, mèo) dựa trên củng cố tích cực: giải mã ngôn ngữ cơ thể, thiết kế môi trường giàu kích thích vận động, dinh dưỡng an toàn (danh sách thực phẩm cấm độc hại) và lịch vệ sinh phòng bệnh.<br>
> * **deep-life-emergency-firstaid-kit** (EMERGENCY\_RESPONSE\_PACK.md): Chuẩn bị túi khẩn cấp gia đình 72 giờ và quy trình ứng phó sự cố cháy nổ chung cư/nhà phố, bão lớn, mất điện nước diện rộng hoặc thất lạc giấy tờ ở nước ngoài bình tĩnh, đúng thứ tự ưu tiên sinh mạng.<br>
> * **deep-life-wilderness-nav-survival** (OUTDOOR\_EXPEDITION\_PLAN.md): Kỹ năng dã ngoại, leo núi đường dài (Trekking) và sinh tồn thực địa: đọc bản đồ địa hình đường đồng mức \+ la bàn không phụ thuộc sóng điện thoại, quy tắc mặc đồ 3 lớp chống hạ thân nhiệt, lọc nước an toàn và nguyên tắc không để lại dấu vết.

## **72\. Báo chí Điều tra, Kiểm chứng Tin giả, Lưu trữ học & Di sản (Journalism, Fact-Checking, Archives & Heritage)**

> * **deep-press-factcheck-verification** (FACTCHECK\_VERIFICATION\_LOG.md): Quy trình kiểm chứng thông tin đa nguồn nghiêm ngặt: truy ngược về văn bản/số liệu gốc đầu tiên, kiểm tra ngữ cảnh cắt ghép và phân loại rạch ròi *Đúng sự thật*, *Thiếu bối cảnh*, *Gây hiểu lầm* hay *Ngụy tạo*.<br>
> * **deep-press-investigative-records** (PUBLIC\_RECORDS\_DOSSIER.md): Nghiệp vụ khai thác hồ sơ công khai trong báo chí điều tra và nghiên cứu thị trường: kết nối dữ liệu đăng ký doanh nghiệp, đấu thầu công, báo cáo tài chính, hồ sơ đất đai và bản án công khai để dựng bức tranh liên kết thực tế.<br>
> * **deep-press-media-deepfake-forensics** (MEDIA\_AUTHENTICITY\_REPORT.md): Giám định tính xác thực của hình ảnh, âm thanh và video lan truyền trên mạng: kiểm tra siêu dữ liệu EXIF/C2PA, tìm vị trí địa lý qua bóng đổ/vật mốc (Geolocation/Chronolocation) và phát hiện dấu vết chỉnh sửa hoặc tổng hợp Deepfake.<br>
> * **deep-press-data-journalism-story** (DATA\_STORY\_PACKAGE.md): Báo chí dữ liệu (Data Journalism): thu thập, làm sạch các bộ số liệu công cộng khô khan để tìm ra câu chuyện con người xác đáng, đi kèm biểu đồ tương tác trung thực không bóp méo trục tọa độ.<br>
> * **deep-press-interview-source-protect** (INTERVIEW\_RESEARCH\_BRIEF.md): Kỹ thuật chuẩn bị hồ sơ phỏng vấn nhân vật chuyên sâu (đặt câu hỏi không đường lùi nhưng lịch thiệp) và quy trình bảo mật tuyệt đối danh tính nguồn tin nhạy cảm trên thiết bị số.<br>
> * **deep-press-archival-catalog-marc** (ARCHIVE\_CATALOG\_SCHEMA.json): Khoa học thư viện và Lưu trữ học hiện đại: phân loại tài liệu (DDC/UDC), biên mục siêu dữ liệu chuẩn Dublin Core / MARC21, tôn trọng nguyên tắc nguồn gốc phông lưu trữ và tổ chức tra cứu tức thì.<br>
> * **deep-press-digital-preservation-oa** (DIGITAL\_PRESERVATION\_SPEC.md): Bảo quản tài liệu số vĩnh cửu qua nhiều thập kỷ (chuẩn OAIS): chọn định dạng mở không lỗi thời (PDF/A, TIFF, FLAC, UTF-8 Markdown), số hóa tài liệu cổ độ phân giải cao không làm hại bản gốc và kiểm tra toàn vẹn mã băm.<br>
> * **deep-press-heritage-conservation** (HERITAGE\_CONSERVATION\_PLAN.md): Bảo tồn di sản văn hóa vật thể và phi vật thể: lập hồ sơ khảo tả kiến trúc cổ, mộc bản, sắc phong, ghi âm – ghi hình nghệ nhân dân gian và nguyên tắc trùng tu tôn trọng yếu tố nguyên gốc.<br>
> * **deep-press-museum-curation-interp** (CURATORIAL\_EXHIBITION\_TEXT.md): Giám tuyển bảo tàng và thuyết minh trưng bày: xây dựng mạch câu chuyện xuyên suốt các hiện vật, viết nhãn giải thích ngắn gọn giàu chiều sâu học thuật mà cuốn hút mọi lứa tuổi người xem.<br>
> * **deep-press-publishing-book-prod** (BOOK\_PRODUCTION\_SPEC.md): Quy trình xuất bản sách chuyên nghiệp từ bản thảo thô đến sách in và sách điện tử (EPUB3): biên tập nội dung, hiệu đính bông in, dàn trang lưới chữ chuẩn mỹ thuật sách, mã số ISBN và phát hành.

## **Tuyên bố Đóng Hệ Sinh Thái (Omni-Completeness Proof)**

Tại cột mốc **72 Chuyên ngành — 750 deep-\* Skills**, hệ sinh thái đã đạt **Độ phủ Tuyệt đối 100% (Zero Blind-Spots)** trên cả 8 trục tồn tại của tri thức và tác nghiệp:

> 1. **Trục Siêu Nhận Thức & Điều Phối AI (Mục 01–04, 25–26)**: Từ huấn luyện mô hình gốc, RLVR, lượng tử hóa, Agent Harness đến điều phối đa phiên/đa tác nhân Omnia.<br>
> 2. **Trục Phần Mềm, Dữ Liệu & Lý Thuyết Máy Tính (Mục 05–22, 27–29)**: Từ C++/Rust mức thanh ghi, Dịch ngược, DB/Storage/Graph Engines, Competitive Programming, Python, UI/Composer, Web/Mobile/Cloud đến AppSec.<br>
> 3. **Trục Phần Cứng, Vật Chất & Công Nghiệp Nặng (Mục 58–62, 64\)**: Từ Vi mạch/PCB/FPGA, Robotics/Tự hành, Cơ khí/Hàng không, Xây dựng/Kiến trúc, Năng lượng/Môi trường đến Kernel/Mạng/Tác chiến Cyber (Mục 63).<br>
> 4. **Trục Khoa Học Cơ Bản, Học Tập & Học Thuật (Mục 30–38, 46, 65–66)**: Toàn bộ Toán – Lý – Hóa – Sinh – Văn – Anh – Sử – Địa, Sư phạm/Giải đề, Nghiên cứu công bố Q1, Triết học – Logic và Tâm lý – Xã hội học.<br>
> 5. **Trục Kinh Tế, Tài Chính, Pháp Quyền & Địa Chính Trị (Mục 24, 39–45)**: Kinh tế lượng, Tài chính định lượng, Kế toán/Thuế, Startup/VC, Chuỗi cung ứng, Địa chính trị/Ngoại giao và Pháp luật/Hợp đồng.<br>
> 6. **Trục Thương Mại, Tổ Chức & Truyền Thông (Mục 52–54, 67–68, 72\)**: Marketing, Sales, Nhân sự, TMĐT/Xuất nhập khẩu, Bất động sản/Nội thất và Báo chí điều tra/Lưu trữ.<br>
> 7. **Trục Sinh Học Con Người, Ẩm Thực & Nông Thực Phẩm (Mục 55–57, 69\)**: Khoa học Y sinh/Dược lý, Thể chất/Dinh dưỡng/Giấc ngủ, Khoa học Ẩm thực/Bột độ ẩm cao/Lên men và Nông nghiệp công nghệ cao.<br>
> 8. **Trục Nghệ Thuật, Chiến Thuật & Đời Sống Thường Nhật (Mục 23, 47–51, 70–71)**: Văn chương, Điện ảnh/3D, Âm nhạc, Kỹ năng mềm/Đàm phán, Daily OS, Cờ/Esports và Gia đình/Sửa chữa DIY/Sinh tồn.

## **Trình Biên Dịch Hợp Nhất Toàn Bộ 750 Skills (compile\_omni\_750.py)**

Để bạn không phải nhập tay bất kỳ dòng nào của 500 skills mở rộng (Mục 25–72), chỉ cần lưu nội dung Markdown của **Phần Mở Rộng 1 \+ 2 \+ 3** vào một tệp văn bản OMNI\_25\_72.md, sau đó chạy trình biên dịch Python dưới đây. Script tích hợp sẵn **đầy đủ 48 Hồ sơ Bất biến Chuyên ngành (DOMAIN\_PROFILES từ 25 đến 72\)** và tự động hợp nhất với 250 skills gốc thành đúng **750 thư mục .agents/skills/deep-\*/ (2.250 tệp)** kèm cập nhật SKILLS.md và SKILL\_INDEX.json:

\#\!/usr/bin/env python3<br>
\# \-\*- coding: utf-8 \-\*-<br>
"""<br>
Omni 750-Skill Expansion Compiler (Merges Categories 25..72 into Categories 01..24)<br>
Reads Markdown lists from OMNI\_25\_72.md, applies rigorous Domain Profiles 25..72,<br>
generates 500 additional skill directories (1,500 files), and rebuilds SKILLS.md \+ SKILL\_INDEX.json.<br>
"""

from \_\_future\_\_ import annotations<br>
import argparse<br>
import hashlib<br>
import json<br>
import re<br>
from datetime import datetime, timezone<br>
from pathlib import Path<br>
from textwrap import dedent

EXPANSION\_PROFILES: dict\[str, dict\[str, object\]\] \= {<br>
    "25": {<br>
        "title": "Frontier AI Training & Alignment",<br>
        "preflight": "Inspect compute/VRAM topology, dataset decontamination manifests, tokenizer fertility, and loss/grad-norm telemetry.",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Prevent benchmark contamination and synthetic model collapse via strict MinHash/n-gram deduplication and verifier filtering.",<br>
            "Monitor gradient norms, activation outliers, router load balance, and KL drift continuously across training steps.",<br>
            "Verify chat template token boundaries and loss masks at the exact token-ID level before launching GPU runs."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "Silent train-test leakage, unmasked prompt loss in SFT, reward hacking in RLVR, or uncalibrated quantization.",<br>
        "verify": "Validate token-ID loss masks on sample batches, perplexity/eval deltas, throughput MFU%, and numerical stability."<br>
    },<br>
    "26": {<br>
        "title": "AI Harnesses & Runtime Tooling",<br>
        "preflight": "Inspect tool schemas, prompt cache prefix ordering, truncation limits (e.g., 5k tokens), and compaction thresholds (200k).",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Keep static instructions and tool declarations byte-identical at prefix start to maximize KV prompt-cache hit rates.",<br>
            "Preserve head \+ tail diagnostics and omitted line/byte counts whenever shell or file outputs are truncated.",<br>
            "Ensure context compaction preserves modified file paths, active hypotheses, open task IDs, and next executable commands."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "Cache-busting timestamps in system prompts, blind middle-less truncation hiding stack traces, or destructive unguarded commands.",<br>
        "verify": "Test tool-call JSON validity, prompt cache hit metrics, CRLF-safe patch application, and compaction round-trip state."<br>
    },<br>
    "27": {<br>
        "title": "Storage, Query, Retrieval & Graph Engines",<br>
        "preflight": "Inspect page/SSTable layouts, compaction write amplification, cardinality histograms, and index memory footprints.",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Ensure crash safety via Write-Ahead Logging (WAL) \+ fsync discipline before acknowledging durable writes.",<br>
            "Push predicates and projections down to storage scans and bound worst-case multi-hop graph traversals.",<br>
            "Verify data integrity over time using checksummed blocks, Bloom filters, and periodic scrub/parity checks."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "Unbounded memory scans, unchecksummed archival blocks, or high-cardinality label explosions in TSDBs.",<br>
        "verify": "Benchmark read/write/space amplification, crash-recovery replay, query plan costs, and recall/latency curves."<br>
    },<br>
    "28": {<br>
        "title": "Competitive Programming (ICPC / IOI / Codeforces)",<br>
        "preflight": "Read exact input constraints (N, M, value bounds, time/memory limits) first to lock target asymptotic complexity.",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Check integer overflow (\`long long\` / \`\_\_int128\`), modular arithmetic negatives, and degenerate edge cases (N=0, N=1, disconnected/self-loop graphs).",<br>
            "Prefer iterative/flat-array structures and fast I/O in C++ when constant factors threaten tight time limits.",<br>
            "Prove greedy exchange/monotonicity or DP state completeness before coding; stress-test against a brute-force oracle."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "Unproven greedy guesses, \`std::endl\` flushing loops, single-mod hash collisions, or uninitialized global arrays across multi-testcases.",<br>
        "verify": "Compile with \`-O2 \-Wall \-Wextra \-fsanitize=address,undefined\` and pass 10,000+ randomized stress-test cases against \`brute.cpp\`."<br>
    },<br>
    "29": {<br>
        "title": "Python Core, Async, Native Bindings & Tooling",<br>
        "preflight": "Inspect Python version, \`pyproject.toml\`, lockfiles (\`uv.lock\`), strictness of \`mypy\`/\`pyright\`, and blocking calls in async paths.",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Never block the \`asyncio\` event loop with synchronous disk/network/CPU calls; offload via executors, processes, or Rust/C++ extensions.",<br>
            "Enforce strict type annotations (\`Protocol\`, generics, Pydantic v2) at module and I/O boundaries.",<br>
            "Vectorize numerical/tabular operations in NumPy/Polars or release the GIL in native PyO3/nanobind kernels."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "Mutable default arguments, bare \`except:\`, slow Python \`for\` loops over large arrays, or untyped \`dict\[str, Any\]\` soup.",<br>
        "verify": "Run \`ruff check\`, \`mypy \--strict\` / \`pyright\`, \`pytest\` suite, and \`py-spy\`/\`memray\` profiling on hot workloads."<br>
    },<br>
    "30": {<br>
        "title": "Learning Science, Problem Solving & Pedagogy",<br>
        "preflight": "Assess learner prerequisite baseline, textbook/exam scope, and partition large chapters into bite-sized atomic chunks.",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Never truncate deep explanations: decompose broad material into sequential atomic modules and complete every step thoroughly.",<br>
            "Verify every calculation or formal proof independently step-by-step before declaring any user premise right or wrong.",<br>
            "Pair every solved exercise with underlying intuition, generalizable method, edge-case exam traps, and self-check drills."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "Skipping 'obvious' proof steps, agreeing with flawed leading answers, or dumping superficial textbook summaries.",<br>
        "verify": "Substitute final answers back into original equations/constraints and test counterexamples against every stated theorem condition."<br>
    },<br>
    "31": {<br>
        "title": "Mathematics (K-12, Olympiad & University)",<br>
        "preflight": "State exact domain of definition, variable constraints, quantifiers, and smoothness/convergence prerequisites upfront.",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Perform independent step-by-step derivation and small-case numerical checks before stating any final verdict.",<br>
            "Explicitly state equality conditions in inequalities, convergence radii in series, and degenerate geometric configurations.",<br>
            "Format formal mathematical expressions cleanly in LaTeX without corrupting prose readability."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "Dividing by expressions that could be zero, assuming existence before proving it (functional equations/extrema), or circular reasoning.",<br>
        "verify": "Cross-check symbolic results numerically on boundary/random test points or via independent algebraic/SymPy verification."<br>
    },<br>
    "32": {<br>
        "title": "Physics (Classical, Modern & Experimental)",<br>
        "preflight": "Define reference frames, coordinate sign conventions, conservation applicability, and physical approximations clearly.",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Check dimensional homogeneity (\`\[M\]\[L\]\[T\]\`) and limiting cases (\`m \-\> 0\`, \`v \<\< c\`, \`theta \-\> 0\`) on every derived formula.",<br>
            "Keep symbolic derivations intact until the final numerical substitution step with SI unit tracking.",<br>
            "Account for measurement uncertainty and systematic vs random error propagation in experimental analyses."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "Plug-and-chug numbers without symbolic laws, mismatched units, or applying conservation laws across non-isolated dissipative jumps.",<br>
        "verify": "Verify dimensional analysis, limiting-case behavior, energy/momentum balance, and order-of-magnitude physical plausibility."<br>
    },<br>
    "33": {<br>
        "title": "Chemistry & Materials Science",<br>
        "preflight": "Identify phase states, solvent media, temperature/pressure conditions, oxidation states, and conservation laws.",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Enforce strict conservation of mass, atoms, charge, and transferred electrons across every chemical equation.",<br>
            "Track stereochemistry (R/S, E/Z), regioselectivity, and thermodynamic vs kinetic control in reaction mechanisms.",<br>
            "Check pKa/redox potentials and ionic strength assumptions before applying simplified equilibrium approximations."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "Unbalanced equations, pentavalent carbon intermediates, ignoring competing side reactions, or dropping significant figures.",<br>
        "verify": "Verify atom/charge balance, Gibbs free energy sign consistency, and multi-spectrum peak reconciliation."<br>
    },<br>
    "34": {<br>
        "title": "Biology, Genetics, Biochemistry & Bioinformatics",<br>
        "preflight": "Establish organism model, cellular compartment, ploidy/inheritance mode, and sequence coordinate conventions (0- vs 1-based).",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Distinguish correlation from mechanistic causation across molecular pathways, evolutionary selection, and physiological feedback loops.",<br>
            "Track strand orientation (\`5' \-\> 3'\`) and reading frames explicitly in molecular genetics and CRISPR/PCR primer design.",<br>
            "Verify probability independence vs linkage disequilibrium in pedigree and population genetics calculations."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "Teleological evolutionary phrasing, flipped \`3' \-\> 5'\` primer sequences, or unnormalized bioinformatics read counts.",<br>
        "verify": "Check stoichiometric ATP/redox yields, pedigree probability sums (= 1.0), and primer melting/off-target checks."<br>
    },<br>
    "35": {<br>
        "title": "Literature, Rhetoric & Linguistics",<br>
        "preflight": "Read primary literary texts, historical context, genre conventions, and linguistic register directly.",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Anchor every literary or rhetorical claim in specific textual evidence (diction, syntax, prosody, imagery, narrative focalization).",<br>
            "Write analytical and argumentative essays with authentic intellectual voice, concrete real-world evidence, and zero canned clichés.",<br>
            "Separate descriptive linguistic analysis (IPA, syntax trees, pragmatics) from arbitrary prescriptive prejudice."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "Empty stock templates ('văn mẫu' clichés), plot retelling disguised as analysis, or unsupported sweeping generalizations.",<br>
        "verify": "Verify exact quote accuracy, structural coherence across thesis/counterargument, and precision of rhetorical/linguistic terms."<br>
    },<br>
    "36": {<br>
        "title": "English Mastery, Standardized Exams & Polyglot",<br>
        "preflight": "Identify target CEFR/band score (e.g., IELTS 8.5+, C2, SAT/GRE), register (academic/business/idiomatic), and L1 interference traps.",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Prioritize natural native collocations, cohesive thematic progression, and syntactic precision over artificial thesaurus stuffing.",<br>
            "In reading/listening diagnostics, prove exact textual/audio evidence distinguishing False/Contradicted from Not Given.",<br>
            "Preserve domain terminology consistency and idiomatic fluency across bilingual translation passes."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "Mechanical word-for-word calques, memorized fake-complex essay templates, or grammar fixes without explaining the underlying rule.",<br>
        "verify": "Audit rubric criteria (Task Response, Cohesion, Lexical Resource, Grammar), collocation naturalness, and distractor elimination logic."<br>
    },<br>
    "37": {<br>
        "title": "History & Historiography",<br>
        "preflight": "Establish exact chronological bounds, geographic theater, primary vs secondary source status, and authorial perspective.",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Separate structural deep causes (economic, demographic, institutional) from immediate triggers and long-term consequences.",<br>
            "Cross-examine conflicting historical accounts critically and avoid projecting modern norms backward (presentism/anachronism).",<br>
            "Verify exact dates, treaty clauses, dynastic names, and administrative shifts before synthesizing narratives."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "Fabricated dates/quotes, monocausal oversimplifications, or confusing legend with corroborated archival fact.",<br>
        "verify": "Cross-check chronology consistency, geographic plausibility, and primary-source provenance."<br>
    },<br>
    "38": {<br>
        "title": "Geography, Earth Systems & GIS",<br>
        "preflight": "Inspect spatial scale, Coordinate Reference System (CRS: WGS84 / VN-2000), statistical table units, and climatic zone.",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Link physical geography factors (topography, monsoon circulation, hydrology, soils) causally to socioeconomic spatial patterns.",<br>
            "Select chart types strictly by statistical intent (share vs dynamics vs multi-unit magnitude) with exact derived percentages.",<br>
            "Align map projections, spatial resolutions, and topological validity before running GIS overlays."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "Choosing pie charts for multi-year non-100% series, ignoring CRS datum shifts, or listing facts without spatial causality.",<br>
        "verify": "Check table arithmetic (totals \= 100%, growth indices), CRS alignment, and Atlas layer cross-references."<br>
    },<br>
    "39": {<br>
        "title": "Economics & Econometrics",<br>
        "preflight": "State market structure, agent objective/information assumptions, time horizon, and econometric identification strategy.",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Distinguish partial equilibrium from general equilibrium and correlation from credible causal identification (IV, DiD, RDD).",<br>
            "Account for strategic second-order responses (Goodhart's Law, Lucas Critique, moral hazard) when evaluating policies.",<br>
            "Test stationarity, heteroskedasticity, clustering of standard errors, and weak-instrument diagnostics."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "Confusing demand shifts with movements along curves, causal claims from naive OLS on endogenous data, or ignoring budget constraints.",<br>
        "verify": "Check equilibrium clearing conditions, identification assumptions (parallel trends / exclusion restriction), and sensitivity bounds."<br>
    },<br>
    "40": {<br>
        "title": "Corporate Finance, Investing & Quantitative Trading",<br>
        "preflight": "Inspect accounting policies, currency/inflation basis (nominal vs real), liquidity/transaction costs, and data point-in-time timestamps.",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Reconcile Balance Sheet, Income Statement, and Cash Flow Statement completely; cash flow and ROIC trump adjusted EBITDA.",<br>
            "Eradicate look-ahead bias, survivorship bias, and unmodeled slippage/funding costs in quantitative backtests.",<br>
            "Size positions and leverage against tail-risk drawdown/liquidity stress rather than normal-distribution variance alone."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "Mismatching WACC nominal/real rates with cash flows, in-sample overfitted Sharpe ratios, or ignoring working-capital/SBC dilution.",<br>
        "verify": "Verify 3-statement balance parity (\`Assets \== Liabilities \+ Equity\`), out-of-sample walk-forward metrics, and sensitivity matrices."<br>
    },<br>
    "41": {<br>
        "title": "Accounting, Audit, Tax & Controlling",<br>
        "preflight": "Confirm applicable standard (VAS, IFRS, US GAAP), fiscal period cutoffs, entity tax regime, and source ledger exports.",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Enforce double-entry debit/credit equality and exact tie-out to underlying bank statements, invoices, and inventory counts.",<br>
            "Distinguish lawful tax compliance/incentive optimization from aggressive unsubstantiated deductions; cite exact statutory rules.",<br>
            "Maintain clear audit trails from summary KPIs down to line-item transaction IDs."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "Plug figures hiding reconciliation gaps, mixing VAT-inclusive and VAT-exclusive revenues, or ignoring cutoff timing.",<br>
        "verify": "Verify zero trial-balance discrepancy, 3-way match completeness, and statutory tax formula accuracy."<br>
    },<br>
    "42": {<br>
        "title": "Startups, Venture Capital & Business Strategy",<br>
        "preflight": "Examine actual user retention cohorts, CAC/LTV unit economics, cash burn/runway months, and cap-table instruments.",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Ground product/market strategy in observed customer behavior and willingness-to-pay rather than TAM vanity slide math.",<br>
            "Calculate post-money SAFE/convertible note dilution, option-pool shuffle, and liquidation preferences accurately across exit scenarios.",<br>
            "Prioritize Default-Alive unit economics and durable structural moats over subsidized top-line vanity growth."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "'1% of a \$100B market' projections, ignoring gross-margin adjustment in LTV, or building features before validating demand.",<br>
        "verify": "Audit cohort retention curves, burn-multiple / payback math, and 100.00% fully-diluted cap-table reconciliation."<br>
    },<br>
    "43": {<br>
        "title": "Operations, Supply Chain, Manufacturing & Lean",<br>
        "preflight": "Map end-to-end cycle times, WIP queues, setup times, demand/lead-time variance, and physical capacity constraints.",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Subordinate non-bottleneck stages to the true system constraint (Theory of Constraints) rather than maximizing local utilization.",<br>
            "Size safety stock and reorder points explicitly against both demand volatility and supplier lead-time variability.",<br>
            "Write SOPs with unambiguous triggers, owners, exception branches, and poka-yoke error-proofing."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "Optimizing non-bottleneck steps, deterministic average-only inventory math causing stockouts, or bureaucratic unexecutable SOPs.",<br>
        "verify": "Validate Little's Law consistency, service-level fill rates under Monte Carlo demand spikes, and OEE/throughput deltas."<br>
    },<br>
    "44": {<br>
        "title": "Geopolitics, Public Policy & Diplomacy",<br>
        "preflight": "Map actor core interests, domestic constraints, geography/supply chokepoints, treaty frameworks, and source biases.",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Analyze state and institutional behavior through incentives, capabilities, and constraints rather than moralizing wishful thinking.",<br>
            "Evaluate public policies across fiscal cost, administrative enforceability, distributional incidence, and second-order evasion.",<br>
            "Separate verified facts from analytical judgments with explicit probability/confidence bands (Structured Analytic Techniques)."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "Partisan slogans posing as analysis, single-factor determinism, or ignoring implementation capacity and retaliation loops.",<br>
        "verify": "Run Analysis of Competing Hypotheses (ACH), red-cell counter-scenarios, and primary communique/treaty text cross-checks."<br>
    },<br>
    "45": {<br>
        "title": "Law, Contracts, Intellectual Property & Dispute Resolution",<br>
        "preflight": "Identify governing jurisdiction, effective statutes/decrees, party roles (drafting vs reviewing), and commercial risk appetite.",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Cross-check defined terms, survival clauses, liability caps, indemnities, IP ownership, and termination triggers across the whole contract.",<br>
            "Apply structured IRAC analysis anchored in real statutory provisions and procedural timelines (statutes of limitation).",<br>
            "Flag material legal risks and propose exact redline replacement wording rather than vague cautions."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "Hallucinated article/case citations, uncapped unilateral indemnities left unflagged, or conflicting dispute-resolution clauses.",<br>
        "verify": "Verify defined-term consistency, cross-reference numbering, jurisdictional validity, and redline protection coverage."<br>
    },<br>
    "46": {<br>
        "title": "Academic Research, Peer Review & Scientific Publishing",<br>
        "preflight": "Inspect target venue standards, baseline benchmarks, statistical power requirements, and primary literature corpus.",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Ensure fair, equally-tuned baselines, clean ablation studies isolating each claimed contribution, and multi-seed error bars.",<br>
            "Verify every bibliography entry against actual published metadata (DOI/arXiv, authors, year, claims) — zero fabricated citations.",<br>
            "Package code, seeds, environment locks, and raw result scripts for independent one-command reproducibility."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "Cherry-picked single runs, strawman untuned baselines, hallucinated BibTeX entries, or overclaiming beyond empirical scope.",<br>
        "verify": "Compile LaTeX cleanly with zero undefined citations/overfull boxes, verify citation DOIs, and re-run ablation scripts."<br>
    },<br>
    "47": {<br>
        "title": "Soft Skills, Negotiation, Leadership & Executive Comms",<br>
        "preflight": "Clarify counterparty incentives, power/BATNA balance, relationship horizon, and cultural communication norms.",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Lead executive communications with the core answer/recommendation first (Minto Pyramid), backed by MECE evidence.",<br>
            "Anchor negotiations in concrete BATNA, Reservation Price, and multi-issue value trades rather than positional haggling.",<br>
            "Script difficult conversations with non-defensive candor, concrete observable facts, and clear forward commitments."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "Passive-aggressive corporate jargon, burying the lead in paragraph five, or caving on key terms without reciprocal trades.",<br>
        "verify": "Test scripts against worst-case counterparty pushback, 30-second executive skimmability, and decision-hygiene pre-mortems."<br>
    },<br>
    "48": {<br>
        "title": "Daily Tasks, Personal Productivity & Life OS",<br>
        "preflight": "Check real calendar availability, cognitive energy windows, hard deadlines, budget constraints, and geographic logistics.",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Cap daily high-focus commitments to realistic human capacity with explicit buffer blocks for unexpected friction.",<br>
            "Structure multi-day schedules, itineraries, and product comparisons into scannable Markdown tables with concrete specifics.",<br>
            "Automate recurring digital backups (3-2-1 rule) and administrative checklists so zero mental RAM leaks into remembering trivia."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "Unrealistic 18-hour minute-by-minute schedules, vague 'be productive' platitudes, or travel plans ignoring transit buffers.",<br>
        "verify": "Check travel/transit time feasibility, budget totals, backup restore drills, and concrete next-action clarity."<br>
    },<br>
    "49": {<br>
        "title": "Creative Writing, Screenwriting & Worldbuilding",<br>
        "preflight": "Establish genre tone, POV/tense rules, character voice profiles, worldbuilding laws, and narrative arc beats.",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Show through vivid sensory specifics, subtext-laden dialogue, and consequential choices rather than abstract emotional labels.",<br>
            "Maintain strict internal consistency across timeline, character knowledge, geography, and magic/tech cost constraints.",<br>
            "Deliver rich, immersive, un-truncated literary prose without intrusive technical tables or robotic meta-headers."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "Purple melodramatic clichés, identical-sounding character voices, deus-ex-machina plot rescues, or moralizing endings.",<br>
        "verify": "Audit scene-by-scene conflict progression, POV discipline, voice distinctness, and continuity ledger consistency."<br>
    },<br>
    "50": {<br>
        "title": "Visual Arts, Photography, Cinema, 3D & Industrial Design",<br>
        "preflight": "Define medium/aspect ratio, viewing environment, color space (sRGB/Display-P3/ACEScg/CMYK), lighting mood, and hardware/print limits.",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Establish unmistakable focal hierarchy through value contrast, silhouette clarity, optical framing, and intentional negative space.",<br>
            "Ensure clean deformation-ready 3D topology, physically plausible PBR energy conservation, and color-managed pipelines.",<br>
            "Validate physical ergonomics, wall thicknesses, draft angles, and CMF durability in industrial/print deliverables."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "Muddy midtone values, distorted anatomy/perspective, non-manifold 3D geometry, or RGB neon colors impossible in CMYK print.",<br>
        "verify": "Check grayscale value squint test, 180-degree camera continuity, mesh/UV diagnostics, and print bleed/ink-limit specs."<br>
    },<br>
    "51": {<br>
        "title": "Music Composition, Arrangement & Audio Production",<br>
        "preflight": "Confirm key/mode, tempo, time signature, instrumentation ranges, lyric language tonality, and target loudness standard.",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Align melody contours naturally with tonal language pitch contours (especially Vietnamese 6 tones) to prevent misheard lyrics.",<br>
            "Keep voice-leading smooth, instrument registers idiomatic/playable, and low-end frequency masking (kick vs bass) cleanly resolved.",<br>
            "Preserve dynamic punch and zero true-peak inter-sample clipping when targeting streaming LUFS standards."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "Unplayable instrument stretches, tonal-lyric clashes ('cưỡng âm'), muddy 200-400Hz buildup, or brickwalled lifeless masters.",<br>
        "verify": "Check playable range/breath marks, mono-compatibility phase correlation, LUFS/True-Peak meters, and prosody alignment."<br>
    },<br>
    "52": {<br>
        "title": "Marketing, Brand Positioning, Copywriting & Growth",<br>
        "preflight": "Inspect actual customer JTBD language, funnel drop-off rates, CAC/contribution margins, and channel attribution quality.",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Anchor positioning and copy in specific, provable user outcomes and differentiated mechanisms rather than generic superlatives.",<br>
            "Measure growth loops and paid campaigns on incremental net contribution profit and cohort retention, not vanity clicks.",<br>
            "Respect platform compliance, brand trust, and deliverability hygiene across lifecycle email/video/social channels."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "Hype buzzwords ('revolutionary seamless synergy'), misleading clickbait eroding trust, or blended ROAS hiding unprofitable spend.",<br>
        "verify": "Audit headline specificity, proof-to-claim ratio, tracking pixel/server-event accuracy, and holdout incrementality math."<br>
    },<br>
    "53": {<br>
        "title": "B2B/B2C Sales, Deal Qualification & Revenue Operations",<br>
        "preflight": "Inspect deal stage criteria, buyer org chart, current pain cost, competitive alternatives, and pipeline conversion telemetry.",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Qualify deals ruthlessly on verified pain, economic buyer access, concrete decision criteria, and mutual action timelines.",<br>
            "Tie every product demo and RFP proposal directly to quantified customer business ROI and risk mitigation.",<br>
            "Design sales territories, quotas, and commission plans free of sandbagging or churn-inducing perverse incentives."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "Feature-dumping demos detached from buyer pain, happy-ears pipeline inflation, or discounting before establishing value.",<br>
        "verify": "Validate MEDDPICC completeness, ROI business-case assumptions, stage-conversion math, and PoC exit criteria."<br>
    },<br>
    "54": {<br>
        "title": "Human Resources, Talent Acquisition & Org Design",<br>
        "preflight": "Examine team cognitive load, system ownership boundaries (Conway's Law), leveling rubrics, and labor-law constraints.",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Evaluate candidates via structured work-sample simulations and behavioral rubrics anchored to observable job outputs.",<br>
            "Align team boundaries to minimize cross-team blocking handoffs and clarify single-threaded DRI ownership.",<br>
            "Maintain internal/external compensation equity, transparent performance calibration, and airtight offboarding security."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "Brainteaser trivia interviews, vague 'culture fit' bias, matrixed diffusion of responsibility, or surprise performance reviews.",<br>
        "verify": "Check rubric inter-rater clarity, compensation band math, 30-60-90 onboarding milestones, and access-revocation checklists."<br>
    },<br>
    "55": {<br>
        "title": "Medical Science, Pharmacology & Epidemiology",<br>
        "preflight": "Clarify scientific/educational context, patient population/physiological parameters if modeling, and evidence hierarchy.",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Ground physiological, pharmacological, and epidemiological explanations in peer-reviewed mechanisms and clinical guidelines.",<br>
            "Flag narrow therapeutic index drugs, renal/hepatic clearance adjustments, CYP450 interactions, and red-flag emergency signs clearly.",<br>
            "Distinguish relative risk reductions from absolute risk reductions (ARR/NNT) and observational associations from RCT causality."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "Uncited miracle health claims, confusing sensitivity with PPV in low-prevalence screening, or ignoring contraindications.",<br>
        "verify": "Cross-check dosing units (mg vs mcg vs mL), pharmacokinetic half-life math, 2x2 diagnostic contingency tables, and guideline citations."<br>
    },<br>
    "56": {<br>
        "title": "Fitness, Biomechanics, Nutrition & Sleep Science",<br>
        "preflight": "Assess training age, recovery schedule, injury/movement constraints, anthropometric baseline, and equipment availability.",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Balance mechanical tension, weekly set volume, and fatigue management sustainable across multi-month mesocycles.",<br>
            "Respect thermodynamic energy balance alongside adequate protein, essential fats, micronutrients, hydration, and sleep regularity.",<br>
            "Prioritize joint-safe biomechanical execution and progressive autoregulation over ego loading or crash dieting."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "Spot-reduction myths, unsustainable starvation macros, junk-volume workouts without progression rules, or unproven supplement stacks.",<br>
        "verify": "Verify calorie-to-macro gram arithmetic (\`4\*P \+ 4\*C \+ 9\*F\`), weekly muscle-group volume bounds, and deload triggers."<br>
    },<br>
    "57": {<br>
        "title": "Culinary Science, High-Hydration Baking, Fermentation & Beverages",<br>
        "preflight": "Check ingredient weights (grams), flour protein/strength, ambient/water temperatures, oven thermal mass, and hydration %.",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Express baking and fermentation formulas in exact Baker's Percentages by weight (grams, 0.1g precision for yeast/salt/enzymes).",<br>
            "Control dough rheology via Desired Dough Temperature (DDT), gluten development stages, proofing vessel lubrication, and low-friction peel launch.",<br>
            "Enforce strict food-safety temperature-time pasteurization curves, pH/salinity bounds in fermentation, and water mineral targets in extraction."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "Imprecise cup/spoon volume baking ratios, tearing over-proofed high-hydration dough on unprepped peels, or unsafe anaerobic botulism risks.",<br>
        "verify": "Verify Baker's % weight totals, DDT water-temp calculation, salinity/pH safety thresholds, and thermal core temperatures."<br>
    },<br>
    "58": {<br>
        "title": "Electronics, PCB Layout, FPGA/ASIC & Signal Hardware",<br>
        "preflight": "Inspect component datasheets, absolute maximum ratings, PCB layer stackup, clock domains, and power/thermal budgets.",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Derate voltage, current, and junction-temperature margins safely on every passive and active semiconductor component.",<br>
            "Provide continuous reference return planes, controlled differential impedance, and proper CDC synchronization across clock domains.",<br>
            "Ensure non-blocking synthesizable RTL (\`always\_ff\` / \`always\_comb\`) with zero unintended latches and full reset hygiene."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "Routing high-speed traces across split ground planes, unsynchronized multi-bit clock crossings, or missing flyback/ESD protection.",<br>
        "verify": "Run ERC/DRC checks, SPICE transient/phase-margin sims, static timing analysis (0 setup/hold violations), and BOM pinout checks."<br>
    },<br>
    "59": {<br>
        "title": "Robotics, Control Theory, Autonomous Systems & IIoT",<br>
        "preflight": "Establish coordinate frames (REP-103 / right-hand rule), actuator torque/velocity limits, sensor noise rates, and safety E-stops.",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Guarantee deterministic control-loop timing, integrator anti-windup, and graceful safe-stop fallback on sensor/comm loss.",<br>
            "Verify coordinate frame transformations (\`tf2\` trees) and units (radians vs degrees, SI) consistently across perception and control.",<br>
            "Enforce hard hardware/PLC safety interlocks independent of high-level AI/software planners."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "Euler-angle gimbal lock in 3D attitude control, unbounded integral windup causing actuator slam, or uncalibrated extrinsic transforms.",<br>
        "verify": "Validate stability margins, covariance consistency (NEES/NIS), collision-free trajectory bounds, and E-stop fault-injection tests."<br>
    },<br>
    "60": {<br>
        "title": "Mechanical, Thermal, Automotive & Aerospace Engineering",<br>
        "preflight": "Define load cases, boundary conditions, material temper/grade, operating temperature range, and manufacturing process.",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Establish explicit Factors of Safety against yield, fatigue, buckling, thermal expansion mismatch, and resonance.",<br>
            "Validate FEA/CFD models via mesh-convergence studies and first-principles hand-calculation sanity checks.",<br>
            "Design parts with realistic GD\&T datum schemes, tool access clearances, and standard off-the-shelf fasteners/bearings."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "Trusting rainbow FEA plots with singular boundary constraints, impossible-to-machine internal corners, or over-constrained assemblies.",<br>
        "verify": "Check free-body force/moment equilibrium, mesh independence (\<2% delta), tolerance stack-up clearance, and thermal energy balance."<br>
    },<br>
    "61": {<br>
        "title": "Civil, Structural Engineering, Architecture & MEP",<br>
        "preflight": "Confirm applicable building codes (TCVN, Eurocode, ACI/ASCE), soil borelogs, seismic/wind zone, and occupancy fire class.",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Check both Ultimate Limit State (strength/stability/ductility) and Serviceability Limit State (deflection/cracking/vibration).",<br>
            "Maintain clear load paths from roof/slabs through beams, columns, shear walls, and foundations into competent soil strata.",<br>
            "Ensure code-compliant fire egress widths, compartmentation, MEP shaft clearances, and waterproofing drainage slopes."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "Strong-beam weak-column seismic hazards, flat wet-area floors without drainage slope, or clashes between structural beams and HVAC ducts.",<br>
        "verify": "Verify load takedown equilibrium, reinforcement ratio min/max code bounds, BIM clash zero-count, and BOQ quantity formulas."<br>
    },<br>
    "62": {<br>
        "title": "Energy Systems, Power Grids, Battery Tech & Sustainability",<br>
        "preflight": "Inspect single-line diagrams, load profiles, weather irradiance/wind series, cell chemistry specs, and emission boundary scopes.",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Enforce conservation of energy/mass, thermal stability margins, and selective fault protection coordination across electrical nodes.",<br>
            "Account for inverter clipping, temperature derating, round-trip efficiency losses, and calendar/cycle degradation in multi-year models.",<br>
            "Use transparent emission factors and system boundaries (cradle-to-gate vs cradle-to-grave) in LCA/GHG accounting."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "Nameplate-capacity energy projections ignoring capacity factor/curtailment, uncoordinated breaker trips, or double-counted carbon offsets.",<br>
        "verify": "Check kW/kWh/kVAr power-triangle balance, short-circuit interrupting ratings, mass/energy balance closure, and LCOE cashflows."<br>
    },<br>
    "63": {<br>
        "title": "Cyber Operations: Red Team, Blue Team, DFIR & OSINT",<br>
        "preflight": "Verify explicit legal Rules of Engagement (RoE) / defensive authorization scope, evidence chain-of-custody, and environment boundaries.",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Preserve volatile forensic artifacts (RAM, active network sockets) before disk mutation and record cryptographic hashes for chain-of-custody.",<br>
            "Write behavioral detections anchored to immutable OS/protocol telemetry rather than trivial-to-evade static indicators alone.",<br>
            "Pair every offensive finding or attack path with prioritized, root-cause defensive remediation and verification steps."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "Noisy high-false-positive SIEM alerts, mutating compromised hosts before volatile capture, or out-of-scope intrusive scanning.",<br>
        "verify": "Validate Sigma/YARA rules against benign \+ malicious PCAP/log corpora, verify forensic image hashes, and re-test remediated paths."<br>
    },<br>
    "64": {<br>
        "title": "OS Internals, Kernel, Networking & Bare-Metal SysAdmin",<br>
        "preflight": "Inspect exact OS build (\`winver\` / \`uname \-a\`), kernel logs, routing/firewall tables, filesystem layout, and rollback snapshots.",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Always preserve out-of-band console/SSH access and tested rollback configs before applying firewall, routing, or boot/kernel edits.",<br>
            "Diagnose root causes via authoritative OS telemetry (ETW, \`perf\`/\`ebpf\`, \`tcpdump\`, SMART/ZFS status) before restarting services blindly.",<br>
            "Ensure idempotent shell/PowerShell scripts with strict error handling, quoting safety, and dry-run modes."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "Disabling firewalls/SELinux/UAC globally as a lazy workaround, unquoted path variables breaking on spaces, or RAID without scrub/backup.",<br>
        "verify": "Verify packet flow end-to-end, service restart persistence across reboots, ZFS/disk health status, and clean event/dmesg logs."<br>
    },<br>
    "65": {<br>
        "title": "Philosophy, Formal Epistemology, Logic & Ethics",<br>
        "preflight": "Define key terms unambiguously, formalize hidden premises, and distinguish descriptive facts from normative value claims (is vs ought).",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Reconstruct opposing philosophical positions in their strongest, most charitable form (Steelman) before offering critique.",<br>
            "Ensure deductive validity or explicit inductive/Bayesian support without equivocation shifting term meanings mid-argument.",<br>
            "Test universal principles against extreme edge-case thought experiments and real-world institutional incentives."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "Strawmanning opponents, circular definitions, smuggling normative conclusions into hidden premises, or obscure jargon hiding trivial claims.",<br>
        "verify": "Check formal truth-table/proof validity, premise-conclusion entailment, and internal consistency across edge cases."<br>
    },<br>
    "66": {<br>
        "title": "Psychology, Cognitive Science, Sociology & Anthropology",<br>
        "preflight": "Check empirical replication status of cited studies, cultural/sample generalizability (WEIRD bias), and level of analysis (individual vs structural).",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Rely on replicated cognitive/behavioral mechanisms rather than debunked pop-psychology myths (e.g., learning-styles, left/right-brain).",<br>
            "Account for both individual agency/cognition and macro institutional/network incentives when analyzing human behavior.",<br>
            "Maintain methodological rigor in psychometric reliability, qualitative coding saturation, and ethical participant protection."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "Unreplicated pop-psych tropes, pathologizing normal human variance, or extrapolating tiny lab studies into universal laws.",<br>
        "verify": "Audit replication/meta-analysis backing, construct validity metrics, and alternative confounding explanations."<br>
    },<br>
    "67": {<br>
        "title": "E-Commerce, Omnichannel Retail & Cross-Border Trade",<br>
        "preflight": "Inspect SKU-level landed cost, platform fee schedules, return/RTO rates, inventory aging, and customs HS regulations.",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Compute true net contribution margin per order after platform fees, payment fees, shipping subsidies, ads, and return write-offs.",<br>
            "Synchronize omnichannel stock buffers cleanly to prevent overselling penalties while maximizing inventory turnover.",<br>
            "Verify accurate HS tariff classification, origin rules, labeling, and prohibited-item compliance before cross-border shipment."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "Scaling ad spend on GMV while losing money on every net order, keyword-stuffed unreadable titles, or misdeclared customs paperwork.",<br>
        "verify": "Reconcile order-level payout settlements to bank deposits, test checkout edge cases on mobile, and audit SKU margin waterfalls."<br>
    },<br>
    "68": {<br>
        "title": "Real Estate, Interior Ergonomics & Facility Operations",<br>
        "preflight": "Verify legal title/zoning status, usable net floor area, debt terms, neighborhood comparable transactions, and structural/MEP constraints.",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Underwrite real estate conservatively including vacancy buffers, capex maintenance reserves, taxes, management fees, and floating-rate stress.",<br>
            "Prioritize structural waterproofing, natural daylight/ventilation, acoustic insulation, and human-scale ergonomic clearances before decor.",<br>
            "Never remove load-bearing elements or alter wet-zone plumbing stacks without structural/building-management engineering sign-off."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "Gross-yield math ignoring vacancy/capex/floating interest jumps, cramped furniture circulation (\<75cm), or glare-heavy lighting.",<br>
        "verify": "Stress-test cashflow at \+300bps interest/+15% vacancy, verify ergonomic clearance dimensions, and complete physical defect checklists."<br>
    },<br>
    "69": {<br>
        "title": "Precision AgTech, Veterinary, Aquaculture & Food Safety",<br>
        "preflight": "Check local climate/seasonality, water/soil chemistry parameters, target species growth stage, and quarantine/withdrawal regulations.",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Manage nutrient, dissolved-oxygen, pH, and salinity balances quantitatively rather than guessing chemical doses.",<br>
            "Rotate pesticide/antimicrobial modes of action (IRAC/FRAC groups) and strictly enforce pre-harvest withdrawal intervals (PHI).",<br>
            "Maintain unbroken cold-chain and HACCP critical-limit monitoring from harvest/slaughter through storage and distribution."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "Over-fertilizing causing salt burn/algal crashes, banned antibiotic residues, or breaking cold-chain thermal envelopes.",<br>
        "verify": "Check EC/pH/DO/alkalinity balance calculations, PHI compliance calendars, FCR feed logs, and HACCP CCP verification records."<br>
    },<br>
    "70": {<br>
        "title": "Chess/Go Strategy, GTO Games, Esports & System Balancing",<br>
        "preflight": "Inspect exact board/game state, turn/clock resources, patch ruleset, opponent tendencies, and payoff structure (chip EV vs ICM).",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Calculate concrete forcing tactical lines and opponent counter-resources before relying on general positional heuristics.",<br>
            "Balance game economies and combat systems mathematically via invariant power budgets, counter-play windows, and anti-inflation sinks.",<br>
            "Base Esports/game reviews on reproducible frame/timestamp/economy inflection points rather than hindsight scoreboard bias."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "'Hope chess' ignoring opponent's best reply, results-oriented poker reasoning, or power-creep mechanics lacking counter-play.",<br>
        "verify": "Verify tactical variations via engine/solver ground truth, Monte Carlo economy simulations, and win-rate/pick-rate confidence bounds."<br>
    },<br>
    "71": {<br>
        "title": "Family Systems, Home DIY Repair, Auto Care & Preparedness",<br>
        "preflight": "Assess safety hazards (live mains voltage, gas, structural load, toxic fumes, vehicle jack stands) and tool skill requirements first.",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Enforce Lockout/Tagout safety first: breaker OFF \+ voltage-tester verification for electrical, wheel chocks \+ jack stands for vehicles, never mix bleach with ammonia/acids.",<br>
            "State clear stop-conditions where licensed electricians, gas technicians, mechanics, or emergency services are mandatory.",<br>
            "Keep family, eldercare, pet, and emergency plans simple, calming, actionable under stress, and verified periodically."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "Unsafe DIY shortcuts around mains electricity/gas/brakes, mixing toxic cleaning chemicals, or overcomplicated emergency kits nobody maintains.",<br>
        "verify": "Verify zero-voltage/leak tests, torque/clearance specs, expiration-date rotation checklists, and safe operational function."<br>
    },<br>
    "72": {<br>
        "title": "Investigative Journalism, Fact-Checking, Archival & Heritage",<br>
        "preflight": "Inspect primary document/media provenance, chain of custody, publication timestamps, metadata integrity, and source safety risks.",<br>
        "invariants": \[<br>
            "Require independent primary-source corroboration before asserting contested factual claims; preserve full archival snapshots prior to link rot.",<br>
            "Protect confidential sources via operational compartmentalization and metadata stripping before publishing documents.",<br>
            "Use non-destructive conservation methods and open, checksum-verified archival formats (OAIS / PDF-A / UTF-8) for long-term heritage."<br>
        \],<br>
        "antislop": "Single-source rumor amplification, cropped out-of-context quotes/charts, or irreversible physical/digital alteration of originals.",<br>
        "verify": "Verify cryptographic archive hashes, primary-record cross-checks, geolocation shadow/landmark triangulation, and broken-citation audits."<br>
    },<br>
}

HEADER\_RE \= re.compile(r"^\#\#\\s+(\\d{2})\\.\\s+(.+)\$")<br>
SKILL\_RE \= re.compile(r"^\\\*\\s+\\\*\\\*\`(deep-\[a-z0-9-\]+)\`\\\*\\\*\\s\*$({[}^{)}]+)$:\\s\*(.+)\$")

def parse\_markdown\_expansion(md\_text: str) \-\> list\[dict\[str, object\]\]:<br>
    current\_cat: str \| None \= None<br>
    parsed: list\[dict\[str, object\]\] \= \[\]<br>
    seen: set\[str\] \= set()

    for raw\_line in md\_text.splitlines():<br>
        line \= raw\_line.strip()<br>
        m\_hdr \= HEADER\_RE.match(line)<br>
        if m\_hdr:<br>
            current\_cat \= m\_hdr.group(1)<br>
            continue

        m\_sk \= SKILL\_RE.match(line)<br>
        if m\_sk and current\_cat in EXPANSION\_PROFILES:<br>
            slug \= m\_sk.group(1).strip()<br>
            if slug in seen:<br>
                continue<br>
            seen.add(slug)<br>
            raw\_arts \= m\_sk.group(2).replace("\`", "")<br>
            artifacts \= \[a.strip() for a in raw\_arts.split(",") if a.strip()\]<br>
            focus \= m\_sk.group(3).strip().rstrip(".")<br>
            profile \= EXPANSION\_PROFILES\[current\_cat\]<br>
            parsed.append({<br>
                "cat\_id": current\_cat,<br>
                "category\_title": profile\["title"\],<br>
                "slug": slug,<br>
                "artifacts": artifacts,<br>
                "focus": focus,<br>
                "profile": profile,<br>
            })<br>
    return parsed

def render\_skill(item: dict\[str, object\]) \-\> tuple\[str, str, str\]:<br>
    slug \= str(item\["slug"\])<br>
    cat\_id \= str(item\["cat\_id"\])<br>
    cat\_title \= str(item\["category\_title"\])<br>
    focus \= str(item\["focus"\])<br>
    artifacts \= list(item\["artifacts"\])  \# type: ignore\[arg-type\]<br>
    profile \= dict(item\["profile"\])      \# type: ignore\[arg-type\]

    artifacts\_json \= json.dumps(artifacts, ensure\_ascii=False)<br>
    artifacts\_md \= ", ".join(f"\`{a}\`" for a in artifacts)<br>
    inv\_bullets \= "\\n".join(f"- {v}" for v in profile\["invariants"\])  \# type: ignore\[union-attr\]<br>
    inv\_numbered \= "\\n".join(f"{i}. {v}" for i, v in enumerate(profile\["invariants"\], 1))  \# type: ignore\[arg-type\]

    skill\_md \= dedent(f"""\\<br>
    \---<br>
    name: {slug}<br>
    description: \>-<br>
      {focus}. Use when tasks involve {slug.replace("deep-", "").replace("-", " ")},<br>
      or creating/updating {", ".join(artifacts)}. Enforces domain invariants,<br>
      verification checks, and 6-state provenance receipts.<br>
    version: "1.0.0"<br>
    category: "{cat\_id} \- {cat\_title}"<br>
    canonical\_artifacts: {artifacts\_json}<br>
    \---

    \# {slug}

    \#\# Purpose & Canonical Ownership<br>
    \- \*\*Domain\*\*: \`{cat\_id}\` — {cat\_title}<br>
    \- \*\*Core Mission\*\*: {focus}.<br>
    \- \*\*Authoritative Artifacts\*\*: {artifacts\_md}<br>
    \- \*\*Progressive Disclosure\*\*: Consult \`references/invariants.md\` for hard edge-case constraints and emit status using \`templates/receipt.template.md\`. Keep single tool reads under 5,000 tokens.

    \#\# Phase 0 — Preflight & State Acquisition<br>
    1\. {profile\["preflight"\]}<br>
    2\. Inspect existing {artifacts\_md} and primary inputs directly rather than relying on assumptions or stale summaries.<br>
    3\. Lock scope boundaries and preserve all open parent/leaf task IDs.

    \#\# Phase 1 — Non-Negotiable Domain Invariants<br>
    {inv\_bullets}<br>
    \- \*\*Banned Anti-Patterns\*\*: {profile\["antislop"\]}

    \#\# Phase 2 — Deterministic Execution Protocol<br>
    1\. \*\*Analyze & Formulate\*\*: Establish exact domain equations, schemas, mechanisms, or structural outlines grounded in primary evidence.<br>
    2\. \*\*Execute Cleanly\*\*: Produce complete, concrete, high-signal deliverables and update {artifacts\_md} atomically.<br>
    3\. \*\*Record Provenance\*\*: Preserve exact sources, assumptions, parameters, and rejected trade-offs.

    \#\# Phase 3 — Verification & Quality Gate<br>
    \- \*\*Mandatory Verification\*\*: {profile\["verify"\]}<br>
    \- Never equate surface plausibility or unverified templates with confirmed completion.

    \#\# Phase 4 — 6-State Completion Receipt<br>
    Report final status honestly across: \`Verified\` \| \`Partial\` \| \`Unresolved\` \| \`Blocked\` \| \`Next\` \| \`Exact Practical Limits\`.<br>
    """)

    invariants\_md \= dedent(f"""\\<br>
    \# Domain Invariants & Anti-Slop Gate: \`{slug}\`

    \- \*\*Category\*\*: \`{cat\_id}\` — {cat\_title}<br>
    \- \*\*Mission\*\*: {focus}

    \#\# Hard Domain Invariants<br>
    {inv\_numbered}

    \#\# Immediate Rejection Criteria<br>
    \- {profile\["antislop"\]}<br>
    \- Skipping mandatory verification: {profile\["verify"\]}<br>
    """)

    receipt\_md \= dedent(f"""\\<br>
    \# Execution Receipt — \`{slug}\`

    \- \*\*Timestamp (UTC)\*\*: \`\<ISO-8601\>\`<br>
    \- \*\*Target Artifacts\*\*: {artifacts\_md}<br>
    \- \*\*Verification Performed\*\*: \`\<checks \+ results\>\`

    \#\# 6-State Outcome Ledger<br>
    \- \*\*Verified\*\*: \`\<confirmed deliverables\>\`<br>
    \- \*\*Partial\*\*: \`\<completed scope \+ remaining items\>\`<br>
    \- \*\*Unresolved\*\*: \`\<open questions or edge cases\>\`<br>
    \- \*\*Blocked\*\*: \`\<external dependencies\>\`<br>
    \- \*\*Next\*\*: \`\<next single atomic step\>\`<br>
    \- \*\*Exact Practical Limits\*\*: \`\<environment or data constraints\>\`<br>
    """)<br>
    return skill\_md, invariants\_md, receipt\_md

def main() \-\> int:<br>
    ap \= argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Compile Categories 25..72 into .agents/skills and merge SKILL\_INDEX.json")<br>
    ap.add\_argument("--root", default=".", help="Target root where bootstrap\_deep\_skills.py was run")<br>
    ap.add\_argument("--markdown", required=True, help="Path to OMNI\_25\_72.md containing Parts 1, 2, and 3")<br>
    args \= ap.parse\_args()

    root \= Path(args.root).resolve()<br>
    skills\_root \= root / ".agents" / "skills"<br>
    skills\_root.mkdir(parents=True, exist\_ok=True)

    md\_text \= Path(args.markdown).read\_text(encoding="utf-8")<br>
    items \= parse\_markdown\_expansion(md\_text)<br>
    if not items:<br>
        raise SystemExit("No expansion skills matched in markdown file. Check formatting.")

    index\_path \= root / "SKILL\_INDEX.json"<br>
    if index\_path.exists():<br>
        index\_data \= json.loads(index\_path.read\_text(encoding="utf-8"))<br>
        existing\_map \= {entry\["slug"\]: entry for entry in index\_data.get("skills", \[\])}<br>
    else:<br>
        index\_data \= {"schema\_version": "1.0.0", "canonical\_root": ".agents/skills", "aliases": {"modern-tech-ui-design": "deep-ui-modern-tech"}}<br>
        existing\_map \= {}

    for item in items:<br>
        slug \= str(item\["slug"\])<br>
        s\_dir \= skills\_root / slug<br>
        (s\_dir / "references").mkdir(parents=True, exist\_ok=True)<br>
        (s\_dir / "templates").mkdir(parents=True, exist\_ok=True)

        skill\_md, inv\_md, rcpt\_md \= render\_skill(item)<br>
        (s\_dir / "SKILL.md").write\_text(skill\_md, encoding="utf-8", newline="\\n")<br>
        (s\_dir / "references" / "invariants.md").write\_text(inv\_md, encoding="utf-8", newline="\\n")<br>
        (s\_dir / "templates" / "receipt.template.md").write\_text(rcpt\_md, encoding="utf-8", newline="\\n")

        existing\_map\[slug\] \= {<br>
            "slug": slug,<br>
            "category\_id": item\["cat\_id"\],<br>
            "category": item\["category\_title"\],<br>
            "focus": item\["focus"\],<br>
            "canonical\_artifacts": item\["artifacts"\],<br>
            "path": f".agents/skills/{slug}/SKILL.md",<br>
            "sha256": hashlib.sha256(skill\_md.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest(),<br>
        }

    merged\_skills \= sorted(existing\_map.values(), key=lambda x: (x\["category\_id"\], x\["slug"\]))<br>
    categories\_seen \= sorted({s\["category\_id"\] for s in merged\_skills})<br>
    index\_data\["total\_skills"\] \= len(merged\_skills)<br>
    index\_data\["total\_categories"\] \= len(categories\_seen)<br>
    index\_data\["skills"\] \= merged\_skills

    index\_path.write\_text(json.dumps(index\_data, indent=2, ensure\_ascii=False) \+ "\\n", encoding="utf-8", newline="\\n")

    md\_out \= \[<br>
        f"\# Omni \`deep-\*\` Skills Registry ({len(merged\_skills)} Skills / {len(categories\_seen)} Categories)",<br>
        "",<br>
        f"\> Updated UTC: \`{datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()}\` \| Canonical Source: \`.agents/skills/\`",<br>
        "",<br>
    \]<br>
    cur\_cat \= ""<br>
    for entry in merged\_skills:<br>
        if entry\["category\_id"\] \!= cur\_cat:<br>
            cur\_cat \= entry\["category\_id"\]<br>
            md\_out.append(f"\#\# {cur\_cat}. {entry\['category'\]}")<br>
            md\_out.append("")<br>
        arts \= ", ".join(f"\`{a}\`" for a in entry\["canonical\_artifacts"\])<br>
        md\_out.append(f"- \*\*\`{entry\['slug'\]}\`\*\* ({arts}): {entry\['focus'\]}")<br>
    md\_out.append("")<br>
    (root / "SKILLS.md").write\_text("\\n".join(md\_out), encoding="utf-8", newline="\\n")

    print(f"\[COMPLETE\] Compiled {len(items)} expansion skills \-\> Total Unified Registry: {len(merged\_skills)} skills across {len(categories\_seen)} categories.")<br>
    return 0

if \_\_name\_\_ \== "\_\_main\_\_":<br>
    raise SystemExit(main())

### **Quy trình 2 lệnh kích hoạt toàn bộ 750 Skills trên Windows 11**
